The Directors are pleased to present this annual report together with the audited            董事欣然提呈本年報及本公司及其附屬公司（統稱
consolidated financial statements of the Company and its subsidiaries (collectively,         「本集團」）截至二零二五年十二月三十一日止年
the “Group”) for the year ended 31 December 2025.                                            度的經審核合併財務報表。


PRINCIPAL ACTIVITIES                                                                         主要業務

The principal activity of the Company is investment holding. The Company’s                   本 公 司 的 主 要 業 務 為 投 資 控 股。本 公 司 的 附 屬 公
subsidiaries are principally engaged in the manufacture and sale of cornstarch, lysine,      司 主 要 從 事 製 造 及 銷 售 玉 米 澱 粉、賴 氨 酸、澱 粉
starch-based sweetener, modified starch and ancillary corn-based and corn-refined            糖、變性澱粉、玉米製副產品及玉米深加工產品。
products. Details of the principal activities of the Company’s subsidiaries are set out in   有關本公司附屬公司的主要業務詳情載於經審核
note 15 to the audited consolidated financial statements.                                    合併財務報表附註15。


BUSINESS REVIEW                                                                              業務回顧

The Group’s revenue is derived from two business segments, namely (i) upstream               本集團的收入來自兩個業務分部，即(i)上游產品及
products and (ii) fermented and downstream products, the production activities of            (ii)發 酵 及 下 游 產 品，有 關 業 務 的 生 產 活 動 於 中 華
which are carried out in the People’s Republic of China (the “PRC”).                         人民共和國（
                                                                                                  「中國」
                                                                                                     ）進行。

Discussion and Analysis on pages 13 to 21. The Risk Review Report set out on                 的管理層討論及分析。風險審閱報告載於第22至
pages 22 to 26 provides a comprehensive review of principal risks and uncertainties          26頁，就本集團所面對的主要風險及不明朗因素
facing the Group.                                                                            進行全面檢討。

primarily driven by lower raw material costs, a condition that proved unsustainable      主 要 受 原 材 料 成 本 下 降 所 推 動，惟 此 情 況 長 遠 而

were facing a series of adverse developments which included rising raw material          者面臨一系列不利發展，包括原材料價格上漲、國
prices, stagnant market demand from both the domestic and international markets,         內外市場需求停滯、持續供應過剩、賴氨酸傾銷調
persistent oversupply, lysine dumping investigations, and increasing competition from    查，以及替代材料帶來的競爭日益加劇。該等挑戰
substitute materials. These challenges have converged within a single year, creating     於 一 年 內 同 時 湧 現，為 行 業 帶 來 極 其 艱 難 的 經 營
an exceptionally difficult operating environment for the industry.                       環境。

FINANCIAL MANAGEMENT AND TREASURY POLICY                                                         財務管理及庫務政策

The Group adopts a conservative approach for cash management and investment in                   本集團對並無指定用途的資金採取保守的現金管
uncommitted funds. The primary purpose of our financial management and treasury                  理 及 投 資 策 略。我 們 財 務 管 理 及 庫 務 政 策 的 主 要
policy is to ensure that the Group has sufficient cash and available banking facilities          目的為確保本集團擁有足夠現金及可動用銀行信
to meet its commitment on one hand and to obtain better returns on the other hand.               貸應付其承諾，以及取得更佳的回報。任何尚未動
Any unused fund is not only placed on short-term/fixed deposits with authorised                  用的資金除會存放於香港及╱或中國的認可金融
financial institutions in Hong Kong and/or the PRC, but also short-term capital-and-             機 構 作 短 期 ╱ 定 期 存 款 外，亦 會 用 作 購 買 短 期 保
reward guaranteed financial instruments.                                                         本保收益型金融工具。


FOREIGN CURRENCY EXPOSURE                                                                        外匯風險

The Directors do not consider the exposure to foreign exchange risk significant to the           鑒 於 本 集 團 主 要 在 中 國 經 營 業 務，而 本 集 團 大 部
Group’s operation as the Group operated mainly in the PRC and most of the Group’s                分交易、資產及負債乃以人民幣計值，故董事認為
transactions, assets and liabilities are denominated in Renminbi. However, foreign               本集團業務並無承受重大外匯風險。然而，出口銷
currencies are received from export sales, and such proceeds are subject to foreign              售 所 收 取 的 貨 幣 為 外 幣，而 有 關 所 得 款 項 於 兌 換
exchange risk before converting into Renminbi. The Group manages foreign exchange                為 人 民 幣 前 須 承 受 外 匯 風 險。本 集 團 並 無 以 訂 立
risk by closely monitoring the exchange rates rather than entering into any foreign              外 匯 對 沖 安 排 的 方 式 管 理 外 匯 風 險，而 是 透 過 密
exchange hedging arrangement.                                                                    切留意匯率管理風險。

Our risk measurement philosophy                                                           我們的風險管理理念

efficiently. Our risk management and internal control systems are not one-off             及 有 效 地 管 理 風 險。風 險 管 理 及 內 部 監 控 系 統 並
arrangements; instead they are integrated into our daily business operations              非一次過的安排，相反，該等系統是我們的日常業
and decision-making process. The objective of upholding a high standard of risk           務 營 運 及 決 策 過 程 的 一 部 份。維 持 高 水 準 風 險 管
management and internal control is to guarantee the sustainability of the Group’s         理及內部監控的目的是保證本集團可持續發展。
development.

control systems. These systems are monitored by the Board through the assistance          體責任。該等系統由董事會在審核委員會、企劃部
of the Audit Committee, Corporate Strategy and Development Department and                 及內部審核部協助下進行監察。
Internal Audit Department.

How we manage risk                                                                        管理風險的方法

Risk and control responsibility must be well-defined and delegated to each staff. Each    我們須明確界定風險及監控責任並落實到各個員
staff must understand their duty and recognise their responsibility. A well-defined       工。各員工均須了解其職責，並深明其責任。界定
control prevention system is a key to success. Our risk prevention mechanism is as        明 確 的 監 控 防 範 系 統 乃 成 功 的 關 鍵。我 們 的 風 險
follows:                                                                                  防範機制如下：


Risk ownership                          The executive management team is assigned the responsibility for managing risks and monitoring
                                        the implementation of risk mitigation policies. In addition, the team heads are required to pledge
                                        themselves to ensure appropriate control procedures are in place within their respective teams to
                                        the Chief Executive Officer once a year.
風險承擔                                    執行管理團隊負責管理風險並監管風險舒緩政策的落實情況。此外，各小組主管須每年向行政
                                        總裁承諾確保其各自的團隊已制訂合適的監控程序。

Monitoring                              A monthly management report in respect to the operation, market and safety issues is circulated
                                        amongst the management team. It allows the management to stay alert on potential risks in
                                        different seasons.
監管                                      每月就經營、市場及安全事宜向管理團隊提交管理報告，令管理層對不同季節的潛在風險保持
                                        警惕。


Compliance review                       Internal Audit Department considers the review areas for the coming year and discusses its plan
                                        with the Audit Committee. It also carries out ad-hoc tasks on specific areas which required by
                                        the Board or the management. The internal audit report will be discussed with the respective
                                        department head and reviewed by the Board respectively.
合規審查                                    內部審核部門考慮來年的審查範圍，並與審核委員會討論其計劃。其亦按董事會或管理層的要
                                        求對特定領域進行臨時審核。內部審核報告將與各部門主管討論，並由董事會審閱。

Dividend Policy                                                                          股息政策

The Board is committed to creating long-term value for Shareholders. As both our         董 事 會 致 力 為 股 東 創 造 長 遠 價 值。由 於 我 們 的 主
major raw material (corn kernel) and our principal finished product (cornstarch) are     要原材料（玉米粒）及主要成品（玉米澱粉）均為對
commodities that are sensitive to global economic conditions, the Group’s financial      全 球 經 濟 狀 況 敏 感 的 大 宗 商 品，本 集 團 的 財 務 表
performance may be affected by fluctuations in the economic cycle. Instead of            現 可 能 會 受 經 濟 週 期 波 動 影 響。本 公 司 並 不 採 納
adopting a fixed dividend payout ratio, the Company aims to provide Shareholders         固 定 股 息 派 付 比 率，而 是 致 力 為 股 東 提 供 穩 定 且
with a stable and regular level of dividends.                                            規律的股息水平。


In determining the amount of dividend, the Company takes into account a range of         於釐定股息金額時，本公司會綜合考慮多項因素，
factors, including:                                                                      包括：


•       future capital and cash flow requirements;                                       •     未來資本及現金流需求；


•       the number and scale of research and development projects;                       •     研發項目的數量及規模；


•       prevailing and projected profitability;                                          •     當前及預期盈利能力；


•       the expected economic environment;                                               •     預期經濟環境；


•       projected capital expenditure and investment opportunities.                      •     預期資本開支及投資機遇。


The Board has determined the proposed final dividend (HK0.98 cent per share) for         董 事 會 參 照 本 公 司 的 股 息 政 策，已 釐 定 截 至 二
the year ended 31 December 2025 with reference to the Company’s dividend policy.         零二五年十二月三十一日止年度的建議末期股息
There is no material variation in the dividend amount as compared with last year         （每股0.98港仙）。本年度股息金額與去年相比無
(2024 final dividend: HK0.98 cents per share).                                           重大變動（二零二四年末期股息：每股0.98港仙）
                                                                                                                。

     2.2   Subsidiaries                                                                     2.2   附屬公司

           A subsidiary is an entity over which the Group has control. The Group                  附屬公司為本集團對其存有控制
           controls an entity when the Group is exposed to, or has rights to,                     權 的 實 體。倘 本 集 團 須 承 擔 或 享
           variable returns from its involvement with the entity and has the ability              有參與實體之可變回報風險及權
           to affect those returns through its power over the entity. Subsidiaries                利，以 及 可 運 用 其 對 該 實 體 之 權
           are consolidated from the date on which control is transferred to the                  力 以 影 響 有 關 回 報 時，則 對 實 體
           Group. They are deconsolidated from the date that control ceases.                      存 有 控 制 權。附 屬 公 司 於 控 制 權
                                                                                                  轉 移 至 本 集 團 日 期 起 合 併 入 賬，
                                                                                                  並於控制權終止日期起不再合併
                                                                                                  入賬。


           Intra-group transactions, balances and unrealised gains on                             集 團 內 公 司 間 交 易、結 餘 及 集 團
           transactions between group companies are eliminated. Unrealised                        內公司間交易的未變現收益均予
           losses are also eliminated unless the transaction provides evidence                    對 銷。未 變 現 虧 損 亦 會 對 銷，惟
           of an impairment of the transferred asset. When necessary, amounts                     有證據顯示交易所轉移資產出現
           reported by subsidiaries have been adjusted to conform with the                        減 值 除 外。如 有 需 要，附 屬 公 司
           Group’s accounting policies.                                                           呈 報 的 金 額 已 經 進 行 調 整，以 與
                                                                                                  本集團的會計政策保持一致。


           Profit or loss and each component of other comprehensive income of                     附屬公司的損益及其他綜合收益
           subsidiaries are attributed to the owners of the Company and to the                    內的各個部份由本公司擁有人及
           non-controlling interests. Total comprehensive income of subsidiaries                  非 控 股 股 東 權 益 應 佔。即 使 會 令
           is attributed to the owners of the Company and to the non-controlling                  非 控 股 股 東 權 益 產 生 虧 絀 結 餘，
           interests even if this results in the non-controlling interests having a               附屬公司的綜合收益總額仍歸屬
           deficit balance.                                                                       於本公司擁有人及非控股股東權
                                                                                                  益。

           of investment. The results of subsidiaries are accounted for by the                    直 接 應 佔 的 成 本。本 公 司 基 於 已

                                                                                                  績。

     2.7   Impairment of non-financial assets                                              2.7   非金融資產的減值

           Assets are reviewed for impairment whenever events or changes                         當有事件出現或情況改變顯示賬
           in circumstances indicate that the carrying amount may not be                         面 值 可 能 無 法 收 回 時，會 就 資 產
           recoverable. An impairment loss is recognised immediately in profit                   減 值 進 行 檢 討。減 值 虧 損 按 資 產
           or loss for the amount by which the asset’s carrying amount exceeds                   的賬面值超出其可收回金額的差
           its recoverable amount. The recoverable amount is the higher of an                    額 即 時 於 損 益 確 認。可 收 回 金 額
           asset’s fair value less costs to sell and value in use. For the purposes              以資產的公平值扣除銷售成本與
           of assessing impairment, assets are grouped at the lowest level for                   使 用 價 值 兩 者 中 的 較 高 者 為 準。
           which there are separately identifiable cash flows (cash-generating                   於 評 估 減 值 時，資 產 按 可 分 開 識
           units). Non-financial assets other than goodwill that suffered                        辨現金流量（現金產生單位）的最
           impairment are reviewed for possible reversal of the impairment at                    低 層 次 分 組。商 譽 以 外 的 非 金 融
           each reporting date.                                                                  資 產 如 出 現 減 值，會 於 各 報 告 日
                                                                                                 期檢討其減值撥回的可能性。

     2.21   Provisions                                                                     2.21   撥備

            Provisions are recognised when the Group has a present legal or                       當本集團因過往事件而須負上
            constructive obligation as a result of past events; it is probable that               現 有 的 法 定 或 推 定 責 任，並 且 可
            an outflow of resources will be required to settle the obligation;                    能 須 流 失 資 源 以 履 行 責 任，而 金
            and the amount has been reliably estimated. Provisions are not                        額 亦 能 夠 可 靠 估 計 時，會 確 認 撥
            recognised for future operating losses.                                               備。未來經營虧損不會確認撥備。


            Where there are a number of similar obligations, the likelihood that an               倘 有 多 項 類 似 責 任 出 現，則 會 整
            outflow will be required in settlement is determined by considering the               體考慮責任所屬類別以釐定履行
            class of obligations as a whole. A provision is recognised even if the                責 任 會 否 導 致 資 源 流 失。即 使 同
            likelihood of an outflow with respect to any one item included in the                 一類別責任內任何一項造成資源
            same class of obligations may be small.                                               流 失 的 可 能 性 不 高，仍 會 確 認 撥
                                                                                                  備。


            Provisions are measured at the present value of the expenditures                      撥備按為履行責任預計所需開支
            expected to be required to settle the obligation using a pre-tax rate                 的 現 值 計 量，計 算 此 等 現 值 使 用
            that reflects current market assessments of the time value of money                   的稅前利率能夠反映對貨幣時間
            and the risks specific to the obligation. The increase in the provision               價值及該責任特有的風險的市場
            due to passage of time is recognised as interest expense.                             評 估。時 間 流 逝 導 致 撥 備 金 額 的
                                                                                                  增加會確認為利息開支。

     3.2   Capital management                                                             3.2   資本管理

           The Group’s objective when managing capital is to safeguard the                      本集團管理資本的目標為保障本
           Group’s ability to continue as a going concern in order to provide                   集 團 可 持 續 經 營 的 能 力，以 為 股
           returns for shareholders and benefits for other stakeholders. In                     東提供回報並為其他權益持有
           order to maintain or adjust the capital structure, the Group may                     人 提 供 利 益。為 了 維 持 或 調 整 資
           adjust the amount of dividend paid to shareholders, return capital                   本 架 構，本 集 團 可 能 調 整 向 股 東
           to shareholders, issue new shares or sell assets to reduce the cost                  支 付 的 股 息 金 額、向 股 東 退 回 資
           of capital. The Group monitors its capital structure on the basis of                 本、發 行 新 股 或 出 售 資 產，藉 以
           gearing ratio. The gearing ratio is determined by total borrowings                   減 低 資 金 成 本。本 集 團 根 據 槓 桿
           to total assets of the Group. During 2025, the Group’s capital                       比 率 監 察 資 本 結 構。槓 桿 比 率 按
           management strategy, which was unchanged from prior year, was to                     本集團的借款總額除以資產總值
           maintain a healthy gearing ratio in order to cope with working capital               釐 定。於 二 零 二 五 年，本 集 團 之
           requirement and unexpected change in economic conditions.                            資本管理策略為維持健康的槓桿
                                                                                                比 率，以 應 對 營 運 資 金 需 求 及 經
                                                                                                濟 狀 況 的 突 發 變 動，有 關 策 略 與

           Neither the Company nor any of its subsidiaries are subject to                       本公司及其任何附屬公司均毋須

12.   INCOME TAX EXPENSE (Continued)                                                     12.   所得稅支出（續）

      The Group’s major business is in the PRC. Under the law of the PRC on                    本集團之主要業務於中國進行。根據中國
      EIT and its Implementation Regulation, the tax rate of the PRC subsidiaries              企 業 所 得 稅 法 及 其 實 施 細 則，中 國 附 屬
      is 25% for both years, except for one subsidiary of the Group which is                   公司於兩個年度之稅率均為25%。惟本集
      recognised as high technology enterprise is entitled to enjoy a preferential EIT         團一間獲認可為高技術企業的附屬公司
      rate of 15% (2024:15%).                                                                  除 外，該 公 司 可 享 有15%（二 零 二 四 年：
                                                                                               15% ）的優惠企業所得稅率。


      No provision for Hong Kong Profits Tax has been made as the Group entities’              由於本集團的實體於兩個年度內並無在
      profit neither arose in nor was derived from Hong Kong during both years.                香港產生或賺取利潤，故並無就香港利得
                                                                                               稅作出撥備。


      Pursuant to the law of the PRC on EIT and its Implementation Regulation,                 根據中國企業所得稅法及其實施細則，對
      non-PRC resident enterprises are levied withholding tax at 10% (unless                   非中國居民企業應收中國企業自二零零
      reduced by tax treaties/arrangements) on dividends receivable from PRC                   八年一月一日起所賺取利潤作出的股息，
      enterprises for profits earned since 1 January 2008. Upon the recognition                按10％的稅率徵收預扣稅（除非稅收協定
      of Hong Kong tax resident status, the Group adopted 5% (2024: 10%)                       ╱安排訂明較低的稅率）。於確認香港稅
      withholding tax rate for PRC withholding tax purposes.                                   務居民身份後，本集團已就中國預扣稅採
                                                                                               納5%（二零二四年：10％）的預扣稅稅率。

      promoting long service with the Group. Staff are required to place housing             長期服務本集團。員工使用有關住屋設施

      housing deposits are interest-free, unsecured and repayable upon the return            證金乃免息、無抵押及於員工交回員工宿
